# Add an interactive hierarchy widget to a highcharter plot

Adds a draggable, resizable hierarchy widget to an existing highcharter
line plot of a hierarchical or grouped time series. Levels can be
reordered by dragging and toggled by clicking, and the series are
restyled accordingly.

## Usage

``` r
add_hierarchy_hc(
  plot,
  structure,
  time_labels = c("auto", "quarter", "month", "year", "date", "none"),
  width = NULL,
  height = NULL
)
```

## Arguments

- plot:

  A highcharter htmlwidget.

- structure:

  Aggregation structure as a string, such as `"(State/Region)*Purpose"`.

- time_labels:

  How to label a numeric x axis holding day numbers. `"auto"` detects
  quarterly, monthly, yearly or daily spacing.

- width, height:

  Output size (any valid CSS unit). When omitted, the original
  highcharter dimensions and sizing policy are kept.

## Value

The highcharter htmlwidget with the hierarchy widget attached.

## Details

The key columns named in `structure` must be kept in the data passed to
[`highcharter::hc_add_series()`](https://jkunst.com/highcharter/reference/hc_add_series.html),
so that every series can be matched to its position in the hierarchy.

## See also

[`add_hierarchy_plotly()`](https://yvesypher.github.io/ggelato/reference/add_hierarchy_plotly.md)

## Examples

``` r
library(highcharter)
#> Registered S3 method overwritten by 'quantmod':
#>   method            from
#>   as.zoo.data.frame zoo 
#> Highcharts (www.highcharts.com) is a Highsoft software product which is
#> not free for commercial and Governmental use

tas_hts <- fabletools::aggregate_key(
  aus_tourism[aus_tourism$State == "Tasmania", ],
  (State / Region) * Purpose, Trips = sum(Trips)
)
tas_hts <- data.frame(
  Quarter = as.numeric(as.Date(tas_hts$Quarter)),
  State = as.character(tas_hts$State),
  Region = as.character(tas_hts$Region),
  Purpose = as.character(tas_hts$Purpose),
  Trips = tas_hts$Trips
)

tas_plot <- highchart() |>
  hc_add_series(
    tas_hts, type = "line",
    hcaes(x = Quarter, y = Trips, group = interaction(State, Region, Purpose))
  )
add_hierarchy_hc(tas_plot, "(State/Region)*Purpose")
```
